# Screen Specification Map

**Phase:** UX 4 - Widget and Screen Specifications

One row for every documented screen. This is the coverage and traceability index for Phase 4: it resolves each `SCR-*` to the wireframe it realizes, the flows it serves, the widgets and Phase 3 system objects it binds, the errors it can raise, and the file that carries its full specification.

This map is derived, not authoritative. Each column restates what the screen block itself states; where the two ever disagree the block wins and the map is the defect.

## 1. Coverage

| Platform | Profile | Runtime | Screens |
|---|---|---|---|
| Patient app | Profile C | React Native | 47 |
| Clinic / Doctor panel | Profile A | Filament panel | 56 |
| Admin panel | Profile A | Filament panel | 62 |
| **Total** | | | **165** |

Every documented screen has exactly one specification block. No screen is specified twice and no block specifies a screen that is not documented; the validator enforces both directions.

## 2. Column meanings

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Screen | The `SCR-*` identifier, unchanged from Phase 1. |
| Name | The screen name, unchanged from Phase 1. |
| Wireframe | The `WF-*` the screen realizes. One per screen. |
| Archetype | The Phase 2 layout archetype. |
| Flows | The `FLOW-*` this screen participates in. |
| Widgets | The `WGT-*` composed on this screen, in registry order. |
| Components | The Phase 3 `CMP-*` bound, including those reached through a widget. |
| Patterns | The Phase 3 `IX-*` bound. |
| Content | The Phase 3 `TXT-*` rules that govern wording here. |
| Accessibility | The Phase 3 `A11Y-*` obligations that bind here. |
| Errors | The `ERR-*` this screen can surface. |
| Phase 1 derivation | Whether Phase 1 recorded the screen as confirmed by a product-owner decision, or as still pending confirmation. Carried forward unchanged; Phase 4 confirms nothing. |
| Specification | The file carrying the full thirteen-section block. |
